<analysis>**original_problem_statement:**
The initial feature request was to update product images in the Medical Devices section of the Pharmacy page. This evolved into a series of complex tasks:
1.  **Authentication Overhaul**:
    *   Implement an email-based patient login system: New users sign up with Email -> OTP -> Set Password. Returning users log in with Email + Password.
    *   Implement a guest login feature. This went through several iterations: from a Skip button, to a mobile+mock OTP system, and finally to a simple mobile number entry with no OTP verification.
2.  **UI/UX Enhancements**:
    *   Add a numerical captcha to the appointment booking confirmation flow.
    *   Remove a WhatsApp + OTP - coming soon option from the login page.
